	<p class="inner-link:underline inner-link:underline-offset-3 font-b0 text-center">
	<strong>With Infobip as a verified Meta provider, you can: </strong></p></div>

<div

	Send messages programmatically to millions of users&nbsp;</li>

	<h2	data-slug="how-the-whatsapp-business-api-works"
	class="font-display text-balance scroll-m-(--global-top-headers-size,4rem) font-a4 font-book text-center"
>
	How the WhatsApp Business API works</h2></div>

<div
	class="scroll-my-64 ib-wrapper-lite max-w-fill border-black mt-0 mb-16 md:mb-24 ml-0 mr-0 pt-0 pb-24 md:pb-48 pl-0 pr-0 [--ib-content-w:auto] is-wrapper-lite:w-auto [--ib-content-justify:stretch] motion-delay-150"
		data-track-block="paragraph"
			data-theme="default"
>
	<p class="inner-link:underline inner-link:underline-offset-3 font-b0 text-center">
	The API acts as a bridge between your business systems and WhatsApp.</p></div>

<div
	class="scroll-my-64 ib-wrapper-lite max-w-fill border-black mt-0 mb-16 md:mb-24 ml-0 mr-0 pt-0 pb-24 md:pb-48 pl-0 pr-0 [--ib-content-w:auto] is-wrapper-lite:w-auto [--ib-content-justify:stretch] motion-delay-150"
		data-track-block="paragraph"
			data-theme="default"
>
	<p class="inner-link:underline inner-link:underline-offset-3 font-b0 text-center">
	The communication flow follows a simple and secure architecture that allows you to automate messaging, handle high volumes reliably, and maintain full control over customer conversations. </p></div>
</div>
</div></div>

<div
	class="scroll-my-64 wrapper-contained w-(--ib-content-w) [justify-self:var(--ib-content-justify)] border-black mt-0 mb-16 md:mb-24 ml-0 mr-0 pt-0 pb-0 pl-0 pr-0 [--ib-content-w:auto] is-wrapper-lite:w-auto [--ib-content-justify:stretch] motion-delay-150"
		data-track-block="columns"
			data-theme="default"
>
	<div class="grid grid-cols-4 max-sm:grid-cols-1 max-md:grid-cols-2 gap-y-12 gap-x-12 auto-rows-auto">
	
<div class="flex [&amp;_div]:mb-0 col-start-1 max-sm:col-start-1 max-md:col-start-1 col-end-span-1 max-sm:col-end-span-1 max-md:col-end-span-1 row-end-span-1 self-stretch h-full [&amp;&gt;*:only-child]:h-full [&amp;&gt;*:only-child&gt;*:only-child]:h-full justify-start justify-self-stretch w-full [&amp;&gt;*:only-child]:w-full [&amp;&gt;*:only-child&gt;*:only-child]:w-full items-stretch [&amp;&gt;*:only-child]:w-full [&amp;&gt;*:only-child&gt;*:only-child]:w-full flex-col pl-0 pr-0 pt-0 pb-0 overflow-visible w-full!">
	
<div
	class="scroll-my-64 ib-wrapper-lite max-w-fill border-black mt-0 mb-16 md:mb-24 ml-0 mr-0 pt-0 pb-0 pl-0 pr-0 [--ib-content-w:auto] is-wrapper-lite:w-auto [--ib-content-justify:stretch] motion-delay-150"
		data-track-block="universal-card"
			data-theme="default"
>
	<div
	class="font-b1 flex flex-col gap-16 sm:gap-24 px-16 sm:px-24 py-24 sm:py-32 rounded-16 sm:rounded-24 in-data-carousel-provider-container:max-w-480 bg-bright-green-300 text-black"
	aria-roledescription="Card">
	<h2	data-slug="trigger"
	class="font-display text-balance scroll-m-(--global-top-headers-size,4rem) font-a2 font-book has-[+a,+button]:mb-auto text-current"
>
	Trigger</h2><p class="inner-link:underline inner-link:underline-offset-3 font-b1 has-[+a,+button]:mb-auto">
	<strong>1. Your business systems</strong> (CRM, ERP, eCommerce platform, contact center, or custom application) trigger messages based on events or customer actions.</p></div></div>
</div>

<div class="flex [&amp;_div]:mb-0 col-start-2 max-sm:col-start-1 max-md:col-start-2 col-end-span-1 max-sm:col-end-span-1 max-md:col-end-span-1 row-end-span-1 self-stretch h-full [&amp;&gt;*:only-child]:h-full [&amp;&gt;*:only-child&gt;*:only-child]:h-full justify-start justify-self-stretch w-full [&amp;&gt;*:only-child]:w-full [&amp;&gt;*:only-child&gt;*:only-child]:w-full items-stretch [&amp;&gt;*:only-child]:w-full [&amp;&gt;*:only-child&gt;*:only-child]:w-full flex-col pl-0 pr-0 pt-0 pb-0 overflow-visible w-full!">
	
<div
	class="scroll-my-64 ib-wrapper-lite max-w-fill border-black mt-0 mb-16 md:mb-24 ml-0 mr-0 pt-0 pb-0 pl-0 pr-0 [--ib-content-w:auto] is-wrapper-lite:w-auto [--ib-content-justify:stretch] motion-delay-150"
		data-track-block="universal-card"
			data-theme="default"
>
	<div
	class="font-b1 flex flex-col gap-16 sm:gap-24 px-16 sm:px-24 py-24 sm:py-32 rounded-16 sm:rounded-24 in-data-carousel-provider-container:max-w-480 bg-bright-green-300 text-black"
	aria-roledescription="Card">
	<h2	data-slug="route"
	class="font-display text-balance scroll-m-(--global-top-headers-size,4rem) font-a2 font-book has-[+a,+button]:mb-auto text-current"
>
	Route</h2><p class="inner-link:underline inner-link:underline-offset-3 font-b1 has-[+a,+button]:mb-auto">
	<strong>2. Infobip acts as the middleware WhatsApp API provider</strong>, managing message delivery, hosting, scalability, and compliance with Meta’s policies. </p></div></div>
</div>
